89.     public sealed partial class Scenario7_DownloadReordering : Page
90.     {
91.         private const int NumDownloads = 5;
92. 
93.         private MainPage rootPage;
94.         private BackgroundTransferGroup reorderGroup;
95.         public ObservableCollection<DownloadOperationItem> downloadCollection { get; } =
96.             new ObservableCollection<DownloadOperationItem>();
97. 
98.         public Scenario7_DownloadReordering()
99.         {
100.             reorderGroup = BackgroundTransferGroup.CreateGroup("{7421B969-18D4-4532-B6BD-22BDABF71C08}");
101.             reorderGroup.TransferBehavior = BackgroundTransferBehavior.Serialized;
102.             this.DataContext = this;
103.             this.InitializeComponent();
104.         }
105. 
106.         protected async override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
107.         {
108.             rootPage = MainPage.Current;
109.             await CancelActiveDownloadsAsync();
110.         }
111. 
112.         private async Task CancelActiveDownloadsAsync()
113.         {
114.             // Only the downloads that belong to the transfer group used by this sample scenario
115.             // will be canceled.
116.             IReadOnlyList<DownloadOperation> downloads = null;
117.             try
118.             {
119.                 downloads = await BackgroundDownloader.GetCurrentDownloadsForTransferGroupAsync(reorderGroup);
120.             }
121.             catch (Exception ex)
122.             {
123.                 if (!IsWebException("Discovery error", ex))
124.                 {
125.                     throw;
126.                 }
127.                 return;
128.             }
129. 
130.             // If previous instances of this scenario started transfers that haven't completed yet,
131.             // cancel them now so that we can start this scenario cleanly.
132.             if (downloads.Count > 0)
133.             {
134.                 CancellationTokenSource cancellationToken = new CancellationTokenSource();
135.                 cancellationToken.Cancel();
136. 
137.                 Task[] tasks = new Task[downloads.Count];
138.                 for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Count; i++)
139.                 {
140.                     tasks[i] = downloads[i].AttachAsync().AsTask(cancellationToken.Token);
141.                 }
142. 
143.                 // Cancel each download and ignore the cancellation exception.
144.                 try
145.                 {
146.                     await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
147.                 }
148.                 catch (TaskCanceledException)
149.                 {
150.                 }
151.             }
152.         }
153. 
154.         private async void StartDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
155.         {
156.             startDownloadButton.IsEnabled = false;
157. 
158.             // Create and start downloads.
159.             await RunDownloadsAsync();
160. 
161.             // After all downloads are complete, let the user start new ones again.
162.             startDownloadButton.IsEnabled = true;
163.         }
164. 
165.         private void MakeCurrent_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
166.         {
167.             Button button = sender as Button;
168.             DownloadOperationItem item = button.DataContext as DownloadOperationItem;
169. 
170.             // Make the selected operation current.
171.             item.download.MakeCurrentInTransferGroup();
172.         }
173. 
174.         private async Task RunDownloadsAsync()
175.         {
176.             // Create a downloader and associate all its downloads with the transfer group used for
177.             // this scenario.
178.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader();
179.             downloader.TransferGroup = reorderGroup;
180. 
181.             // Validating the URI is required since it was received from an untrusted source (user
182.             // input).
183.             // The URI is validated by calling Uri.TryCreate() that will return 'false' for strings
184.             // that are not valid URIs.
185.             // Note that when enabling the text box users may provide URIs to machines on the
186.             // intranet that require the "Private Networks (Client and Server)" capability.
187.             string remoteAddress = remoteAddressField.Text.Trim();
188.             Uri tmpUri;
189.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(remoteAddress, UriKind.Absolute, out tmpUri))
190.             {
191.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
192.                 return;
193.             }
194. 
195.             string fileName = fileNameField.Text.Trim();
196.             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fileName))
197.             {
198.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("A local file name is required.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
199.                 return;
200.             }
201. 
202.             // Try to create some downloads.
203.             DownloadOperation[] downloads = new DownloadOperation[NumDownloads];
204.             try
205.             {
206.                 for (int i = 0; i < NumDownloads; i++)
207.                 {
208.                     string currRemoteAddress = $"{remoteAddress}?id={i}";
209.                     string currFileName = $"{i}.{fileName}";
210.                     downloads[i] = await CreateDownload(downloader, currRemoteAddress, currFileName);
211.                 }
212.             }
213.             catch (FileNotFoundException ex)
214.             {
215.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"Error while creating file: {ex.Message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
216.                 return;
217.             }
218. 
219.             // Once all downloads have been created, start them.
220.             Task[] downloadTasks = new Task[downloads.Length];
221.             downloadCollection.Clear();
222.             for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Length; i++)
223.             {
224.                 DownloadOperationItem item = new DownloadOperationItem(downloads[i]);
225.                 downloadTasks[i] = DownloadAsync(item);
226.                 downloadCollection.Add(item);
227.             }
228. 
229.             await Task.WhenAll(downloadTasks);
230.         }
231. 
232.         private async Task<DownloadOperation> CreateDownload(
233.             BackgroundDownloader downloader,
234.             string remoteAddress,
235.             string fileName)
236.         {
237.             Uri source = new Uri(remoteAddress);
238. 
239.             StorageFile destinationFile;
240.             try
241.             {
242.                 destinationFile = await KnownFolders.PicturesLibrary.CreateFileAsync(
243.                     fileName,
244.                     CreationCollisionOption.GenerateUniqueName);
245.             }
246.             catch (FileNotFoundException ex)
247.             {
248.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"Error while creating file: {ex.Message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
249.                 throw;
250.             }
251. 
252.             return downloader.CreateDownload(source, destinationFile);
253.         }
254. 
255.         private async Task DownloadAsync(DownloadOperationItem item)
256.         {
257.             Progress<DownloadOperation> progressCallback = new Progress<DownloadOperation>(
258.                 (operation) => DownloadProgress(item));
259. 
260.             try
261.             {
262.                 await item.download.StartAsync().AsTask(progressCallback);
263. 
264.                 item.stateText = item.download.Progress.Status.ToString();
265.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(
266.                     $"Downloading {item.download.ResultFile.Name} completed.",
267.                     NotifyType.StatusMessage);
268.             }
269.             catch (Exception ex)
270.             {
271.                 // Ignore canceled downloads since they are not displayed.
272.                 if (item.download.Progress.Status != BackgroundTransferStatus.Canceled)
273.                 {
274.                     // Ensure that we reach 100% even for errors.
275.                     item.percentComplete = 100;
276.                     item.stateText = item.download.Progress.Status.ToString();
277.                     if (!IsWebException("Execution error", ex, item.download))
278.                     {
279.                         throw;
280.                     }
281.                 }
282.             }
283.         }
284. 
285.         private void DownloadProgress(DownloadOperationItem item)
286.         {
287.             BackgroundDownloadProgress currentProgress = item.download.Progress;
288.             BackgroundTransferStatus status = currentProgress.Status;
289.             int percentComplete = 0;
290. 
291.             if (currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive > 0)
292.             {
293.                 percentComplete = (int)((currentProgress.BytesReceived * 100) / currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive);
294.             }
295. 
296.             // TODO[migrate-014]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
297.             var ignore = this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
298.             {
299.                 item.stateText = status.ToString();
300.                 item.percentComplete = percentComplete;
301.             });
302.         }
303. 
304.         private bool IsWebException(string title, Exception ex, DownloadOperation download = null)
305.         {
306.             WebErrorStatus error = BackgroundTransferError.GetStatus(ex.HResult);
307.             bool result = (error != WebErrorStatus.Unknown);
308.             string message = result ? error.ToString() : ex.Message;
309. 
310.             if (download == null)
311.             {
312.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
313.             }
314.             else
315.             {
316.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{download.ResultFile.Name} - {title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
317.             }
318. 
319.             return result;
320.         }
321.     }
322. }

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Globalization;
15. using System.Linq;
16. using System.Text;
17. using System.Threading.Tasks;
18. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
19. using Windows.Data.Xml.Dom;
20. using Windows.Networking.BackgroundTransfer;
21. using Windows.Storage;
22. using Windows.UI.Notifications;
23. 
24. namespace Tasks
25. {
26.     // TODO[migrate-015]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
27.     public sealed class CompletionGroupTask : IBackgroundTask
28.     {
29.         // Completion groups allow us to immediately take action after a set of downloads completes.
30.         // In this sample, the server intentionally replies with an error status for some of the downloads.
31.         // Based on the trigger details, we can determine which of the downloads have failed and try them again
32.         // using a new completion group.
33.         public async void Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
34.         {
35.             // The background task awaits on an asynchronous task (RetryDownloadsAsync). Take a deferral
36.             // to delay the background task from closing prematurely while the asynchronous code is still running.
37.             BackgroundTaskDeferral deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
38. 
39.             BackgroundTransferCompletionGroupTriggerDetails details = taskInstance.TriggerDetails
40.                 as BackgroundTransferCompletionGroupTriggerDetails;
41. 
42.             if (details == null)
43.             {
44.                 // This task was not triggered by a completion group.
45.                 deferral.Complete();
46.                 return;
47.             }
48. 
49.             List<DownloadOperation> failedDownloads = new List<DownloadOperation>();
50.             int succeeded = 0;
51.             foreach (DownloadOperation download in details.Downloads)
52.             {
53.                 if (IsFailed(download))
54.                 {
55.                     failedDownloads.Add(download);
56.                 }
57.                 else
58.                 {
59.                     succeeded++;
60.                 }
61.             }
62. 
63.             InvokeSimpleToast(succeeded, failedDownloads.Count);
64. 
65.             if (failedDownloads.Count > 0)
66.             {
67.                 await RetryDownloadsAsync(failedDownloads);
68.             }
69. 
70.             deferral.Complete();
71.         }
72. 
73.         private bool IsFailed(DownloadOperation download)
74.         {
75.             BackgroundTransferStatus status = download.Progress.Status;
76.             if (status == BackgroundTransferStatus.Error || status == BackgroundTransferStatus.Canceled)
77.             {
78.                 return true;
79.             }
80. 
81.             ResponseInformation response = download.GetResponseInformation();
82.             if (response == null || response.StatusCode != 200)
83.             {
84.                 return true;
85.             }
86. 
87.             return false;
88.         }
89.         private async Task RetryDownloadsAsync(IEnumerable<DownloadOperation> downloadsToRetry)
90.         {
91.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = CompletionGroupTask.CreateBackgroundDownloader();
92. 
93.             foreach (DownloadOperation downloadToRetry in downloadsToRetry)
94.             {
95.                 // Retry with the same uri, but save to a different file name.
96.                 string originalName = downloadToRetry.ResultFile.Name;
97.                 string newName = originalName.Insert(originalName.LastIndexOf('.'), "_retried");
98.                 await downloadToRetry.ResultFile.RenameAsync(newName, NameCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
99. 
100.                 DownloadOperation download = downloader.CreateDownload(downloadToRetry.RequestedUri, downloadToRetry.ResultFile);
101. 
102.                 // We do not await on background transfer asynchronous tasks, since await will only 
103.                 // finish after the entire transfer is complete, which may take a very long time. 
104.                 // The completion of these downloads will be handled by a future CompletionGroupTask instance.
105.                 Task<DownloadOperation> startTask = download.StartAsync().AsTask();
106.             }
107. 
108.             downloader.CompletionGroup.Enable();
109.         }
110. 
111.         public void InvokeSimpleToast(int succeeded, int failed)
112.         {
113.             // TODO[migrate-016]: see PATTERNS.md#notifications
114.             XmlDocument toastXml = ToastNotificationManager.GetTemplateContent(ToastTemplateType.ToastImageAndText02);
115. 
116.             XmlNodeList stringElements = toastXml.GetElementsByTagName("text");
117.             stringElements.Item(0).AppendChild(toastXml.CreateTextNode(String.Format(
118.                 CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
119.                 "{0} downloads succeeded.",
120.                 succeeded)));
121.             stringElements.Item(1).AppendChild(toastXml.CreateTextNode(String.Format(
122.                 CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
123.                 "{0} downloads failed.",
124.                 failed)));
125. 
126.             ToastNotification toast = new ToastNotification(toastXml);
127.             // TODO[migrate-017]: see PATTERNS.md#notifications
128.             ToastNotificationManager.CreateToastNotifier().Show(toast);
129.         }
130. 
131.         public static BackgroundDownloader CreateBackgroundDownloader()
132.         {
133.             BackgroundTransferCompletionGroup completionGroup = new BackgroundTransferCompletionGroup();
134. 
135.             // TODO[migrate-018]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
136.             BackgroundTaskBuilder builder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
137.             builder.TaskEntryPoint = "Tasks.CompletionGroupTask";
138.             builder.SetTrigger(completionGroup.Trigger);
139. 
140.             // The system automatically unregisters the BackgroundTransferCompletionGroup task when it triggers.
141.             // You do not need to unregister it explicitly.
142.             BackgroundTaskRegistration taskRegistration = builder.Register();
143. 
144.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader(completionGroup);
145. 
146.             return downloader;
147.         }
148. 
149.     }

71.     <PublishTrimmed Condition="'$(Configuration)' != 'Debug'">True</PublishTrimmed>
72.   </PropertyGroup>

<a id="threading"></a>
## Threading: CoreDispatcher → DispatcherQueueUWP (replace this):```csharp
await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => StatusText.Text = "Done");
```WinUI 3:```csharp
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => StatusText.Text = "Done");
DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(DispatcherQueuePriority.High, () => ProgressBar.Value = 100);
```Cache the queue off the UI thread via `DispatcherQueue.GetForCurrentThread()`. UWP's ASTA reentrancy protection is gone — watch for reentrancy in async code that pumps messages. See the official [threading guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/threading).
---
<a id="pickers"></a>
## Pickers and Win32 SurfacesUWP pickers infer the active window. WinUI 3 desktop pickers must be initialized explicitly or `ShowAsync` throws `COMException`.```csharp
var picker = new FileOpenPicker();
picker.FileTypeFilter.Add(".txt");
var hwnd = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(App.MainWindow);
WinRT.Interop.InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(picker, hwnd);
var file = await picker.PickSingleFileAsync();
```Apply the same `InitializeWithWindow.Initialize(obj, hwnd)` pattern to `FolderPicker`, `FileSavePicker`, `DataTransferManager` (Share), `PrintManager`, and other UI surfaces that target a window.
<exited with exit code 0>
--- RESULT ---
<a id="notifications"></a>
## Notifications| UWP | WinUI 3 / WinAppSDK |
|-----|---------------------|
| `ToastNotificationManager` (Windows.UI.Notifications) | `AppNotificationManager` (Microsoft.Windows.AppNotifications) |
| WNS push via `PushNotificationChannelManager` | `PushNotificationManager` (Microsoft.Windows.PushNotifications) |See the [toast notifications guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/toast-notifications) and [push notifications guide](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/notifications).
---
<a id="background-tasks"></a>
## Background Tasks`IBackgroundTask` / `BackgroundTaskRegistration` are not the recommended model. Use the WinAppSDK [`BackgroundTaskBuilder`](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/windows-app-sdk/api/winrt/microsoft.windows.applicationmodel.background.backgroundtaskbuilder) (introduced in 1.7), or move the work to push-driven activation / Windows Task Scheduler. See the [background task migration strategy](https://learn.microsoft.com/windows/apps/windows-app-sdk/migrate-to-windows-app-sdk/guides/background-task-migration-strategy).**If you keep the classic out-of-process model** (`Windows.ApplicationModel.Background.BackgroundTaskBuilder` with a string `TaskEntryPoint` such as `"Tasks.SampleBackgroundTask"` + `builder.Register()`) — which is the low-risk way to preserve existing behavior — then two things are **runtime prerequisites**, not optional:1. The app must be **packaged** (keep `Package.appxmanifest`), and the **build** manifest must declare an `<Extension Category="windows.backgroundTasks" EntryPoint="<Namespace>.<TaskClass>">` for **every** `TaskEntryPoint` string used in code. This declaration lives in the UWP source manifest — carry it into the WinUI 3 project's root `Package.appxmanifest` (see the manifest checklist below). If it is missing, `builder.Register()` **throws at runtime** (`0x80070032`/class-not-registered) even though the app builds and launches fine.   ```xml
   <Application ...>
     <Extensions>
       <Extension Category="windows.backgroundTasks" EntryPoint="Tasks.SampleBackgroundTask">
         <BackgroundTasks>
           <Task Type="systemEvent" />
           <Task Type="timer" />
         </BackgroundTasks>
       </Extension>
     </Extensions>
   </Application>
   ```2. Wrap `Register()` in `try/catch` and surface the failure in the UI (e.g. the Status text). The SDK samples call `Register()` with no guard, so a missing-declaration failure otherwise presents as a **silent dead control** (the button click does nothing, Status never changes) — the hardest kind of regression to notice because there is no build error and no crash.
<exited with exit code 0>

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Globalization;
15. using System.IO;
16. using System.Threading;
17. using System.Threading.Tasks;
18. using Windows.Networking.BackgroundTransfer;

20. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
21. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
22. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
23. using Windows.Web;

25. namespace SDKTemplate
26. {
27.     /// <summary>
28.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
29.     /// </summary>
30.     public sealed partial class Scenario1_Download : Page, IDisposable
31.     {
32.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
33.         // as NotifyUser()
34.         private MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
35. 
36.         private List<DownloadOperation> activeDownloads;
37.         private CancellationTokenSource cts;
38. 
39.         public Scenario1_Download()
40.         {
41.             cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
42. 
43.             this.InitializeComponent();
44.         }
45. 
46.         public void Dispose()
47.         {
48.             if (cts != null)
49.             {
50.                 cts.Dispose();
51.                 cts = null;
52.             }
53. 
54.             GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
55.         }
56. 
57.         /// <summary>
58.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
59.         /// </summary>
60.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
61.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
62.         protected async override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
63.         {
64.             // An application must enumerate downloads when it gets started to prevent stale downloads/uploads.
65.             // Typically this can be done in the App class by overriding OnLaunched() and checking for
66.             // "args.Kind == ActivationKind.Launch" to detect an actual app launch.
67.             // We do it here in the sample to keep the sample code consolidated.
68.             await DiscoverActiveDownloadsAsync();
69.         }
70. 
71. 
72.         // Enumerate the downloads that were going on in the background while the app was closed.
73.         private async Task DiscoverActiveDownloadsAsync()
74.         {
75.             activeDownloads = new List<DownloadOperation>();
76. 
77.             IReadOnlyList<DownloadOperation> downloads = null;
78.             try
79.             {
80.                 downloads = await BackgroundDownloader.GetCurrentDownloadsAsync();
81.             }
82.             catch (Exception ex)
83.             {
84.                 if (!IsExceptionHandled("Discovery error", ex))
85.                 {
86.                     throw;
87.                 }
88.                 return;
89.             }
90. 
91.             Log("Loading background downloads: " + downloads.Count);
92. 
93.             if (downloads.Count > 0)
94.             {
95.                 List<Task> tasks = new List<Task>();
96.                 foreach (DownloadOperation download in downloads)
97.                 {
98.                     Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
99.                         "Discovered background download: {0}, Status: {1}", download.Guid,
100.                         download.Progress.Status));
101. 
102.                     // Attach progress and completion handlers.
103.                     tasks.Add(HandleDownloadAsync(download, false));
104.                 }
105. 
106.                 // Don't await HandleDownloadAsync() in the foreach loop since we would attach to the second
107.                 // download only when the first one completed; attach to the third download when the second one
108.                 // completes etc. We want to attach to all downloads immediately.
109.                 // If there are actions that need to be taken once downloads complete, await tasks here, outside
110.                 // the loop.
111.                 await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
112.             }
113.         }
114. 
115.         private async void StartDownload(BackgroundTransferPriority priority)
116.         {
117.             // Validating the URI is required since it was received from an untrusted source (user input).
118.             // The URI is validated by calling Uri.TryCreate() that will return 'false' for strings that are not valid URIs.
119.             // Note that when enabling the text box users may provide URIs to machines on the intrAnet that require
120.             // the "Private Networks (Client and Server)" capability.
121.             Uri source;
122.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(serverAddressField.Text.Trim(), UriKind.Absolute, out source))
123.             {
124.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
125.                 return;
126.             }
127. 
128.             string destination = fileNameField.Text.Trim();
129. 
130.             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destination))
131.             {
132.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("A local file name is required.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
133.                 return;
134.             }
135. 
136.             StorageFile destinationFile;
137.             try
138.             {
139.                 StorageFolder picturesLibrary = await KnownFolders.GetFolderForUserAsync(null /* current user */, KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
140.                 destinationFile = await picturesLibrary.CreateFileAsync(
141.                     destination,
142.                     CreationCollisionOption.GenerateUniqueName);
143.             }
144.             catch (FileNotFoundException ex)
145.             {
146.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Error while creating file: " + ex.Message, NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
147.                 return;
148.             }
149. 
150.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader();
151.             DownloadOperation download = downloader.CreateDownload(source, destinationFile);
152. 
153.             Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Downloading {0} to {1} with {2} priority, {3}",
154.                 source.AbsoluteUri, destinationFile.Name, priority, download.Guid));
155. 
156.             download.Priority = priority;
157. 
158.             // Attach progress and completion handlers.
159.             await HandleDownloadAsync(download, true);
160.         }
161. 
162.         private void StartDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
163.         {
164.             StartDownload(BackgroundTransferPriority.Default);
165.         }
166. 
167.         private void StartHighPriorityDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
168.         {
169.             StartDownload(BackgroundTransferPriority.High);
170.         }
171. 
172.         private void PauseAll_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
173.         {
174.             Log("Downloads: " + activeDownloads.Count);
175. 
176.             foreach (DownloadOperation download in activeDownloads)
177.             {
178.                 // DownloadOperation.Progress is updated in real-time while the operation is ongoing. Therefore,
179.                 // we must make a local copy so that we can have a consistent view of that ever-changing state
180.                 // throughout this method's lifetime.
181.                 BackgroundDownloadProgress currentProgress = download.Progress;
182. 
183.                 if (currentProgress.Status == BackgroundTransferStatus.Running)
184.                 {
185.                     download.Pause();
186.                     Log("Paused: " + download.Guid);
187.                 }
188.                 else
189.                 {
190.                     Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Skipped: {0}, Status: {1}", download.Guid,
191.                         currentProgress.Status));
192.                 }
193.             }
194.         }
195. 
196.         private void ResumeAll_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
197.         {
198.             Log("Downloads: " + activeDownloads.Count);
199. 
200.             foreach (DownloadOperation download in activeDownloads)
201.             {
202.                 // DownloadOperation.Progress is updated in real-time while the operation is ongoing. Therefore,
203.                 // we must make a local copy so that we can have a consistent view of that ever-changing state
204.                 // throughout this method's lifetime.
205.                 BackgroundDownloadProgress currentProgress = download.Progress;
206. 
207.                 if (currentProgress.Status == BackgroundTransferStatus.PausedByApplication)
208.                 {
209.                     download.Resume();
210.                     Log("Resumed: " + download.Guid);
211.                 }
212.                 else
213.                 {
214.                     Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Skipped: {0}, Status: {1}", download.Guid,
215.                         currentProgress.Status));
216.                 }
217.             }
218.         }
219. 
220.         private void CancelAll_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
221.         {
222.             Log("Canceling Downloads: " + activeDownloads.Count);
223. 
224.             cts.Cancel();
225.             cts.Dispose();
226. 
227.             // Re-create the CancellationTokenSource and activeDownloads for future downloads.
228.             cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
229.             activeDownloads = new List<DownloadOperation>();
230.         }
231. 
232.         // Note that this event is invoked on a background thread, so we cannot access the UI directly.
233.         private void DownloadProgress(DownloadOperation download)
234.         {
235.             // DownloadOperation.Progress is updated in real-time while the operation is ongoing. Therefore,
236.             // we must make a local copy so that we can have a consistent view of that ever-changing state
237.             // throughout this method's lifetime.
238.             BackgroundDownloadProgress currentProgress = download.Progress;
239. 
240.             MarshalLog(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Progress: {0}, Status: {1}", download.Guid,
241.                 currentProgress.Status));
242. 
243.             double percent = 100;
244.             if (currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive > 0)
245.             {
246.                 percent = currentProgress.BytesReceived * 100 / currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive;
247.             }
248. 
249.             MarshalLog(String.Format(
250.                 CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
251.                 " - Transferred bytes: {0} of {1}, {2}%",
252.                 currentProgress.BytesReceived,
253.                 currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive,
254.                 percent));
255. 
256.             if (currentProgress.HasRestarted)
257.             {
258.                 MarshalLog(" - Download restarted");
259.             }
260. 
261.             if (currentProgress.HasResponseChanged)
262.             {
263.                 // We have received new response headers from the server.
264.                 // Be aware that GetResponseInformation() returns null for non-HTTP transfers (e.g., FTP).
265.                 ResponseInformation response = download.GetResponseInformation();
266.                 int headersCount = response != null ? response.Headers.Count : 0;
267. 
268.                 MarshalLog(" - Response updated; Header count: " + headersCount);
269. 
270.                 // If you want to stream the response data this is a good time to start.
271.                 // download.GetResultStreamAt(0);
272.             }
273.         }
274. 
275.         private async Task HandleDownloadAsync(DownloadOperation download, bool start)
276.         {
277.             try
278.             {
279.                 LogStatus("Running: " + download.Guid, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
280. 
281.                 // Store the download so we can pause/resume.
282.                 activeDownloads.Add(download);
283. 
284.                 Progress<DownloadOperation> progressCallback = new Progress<DownloadOperation>(DownloadProgress);
285.                 if (start)
286.                 {
287.                     // Start the download and attach a progress handler.
288.                     await download.StartAsync().AsTask(cts.Token, progressCallback);
289.                 }
290.                 else
291.                 {
292.                     // The download was already running when the application started, re-attach the progress handler.
293.                     await download.AttachAsync().AsTask(cts.Token, progressCallback);
294.                 }
295. 
296.                 ResponseInformation response = download.GetResponseInformation();
297. 
298.                 // GetResponseInformation() returns null for non-HTTP transfers (e.g., FTP).
299.                 string statusCode = response != null ? response.StatusCode.ToString() : String.Empty;
300. 
301.                 LogStatus(
302.                     String.Format(
303.                         CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
304.                         "Completed: {0}, Status Code: {1}",
305.                         download.Guid,
306.                         statusCode),
307.                     NotifyType.StatusMessage);
308.             }
309.             catch (TaskCanceledException)
310.             {
311.                 LogStatus("Canceled: " + download.Guid, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
312.             }
313.             catch (Exception ex)
314.             {
315.                 if (!IsExceptionHandled("Execution error", ex, download))
316.                 {
317.                     throw;
318.                 }
319.             }
320.             finally
321.             {
322.                 activeDownloads.Remove(download);
323.             }
324.         }
325. 
326.         private bool IsExceptionHandled(string title, Exception ex, DownloadOperation download = null)
327.         {
328.             WebErrorStatus error = BackgroundTransferError.GetStatus(ex.HResult);
329.             if (error == WebErrorStatus.Unknown)
330.             {
331.                 return false;
332.             }
333. 
334.             if (download == null)
335.             {
336.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Error: {0}: {1}", title, error),
337.                     NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
338.             }
339.             else
340.             {
341.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Error: {0} - {1}: {2}", download.Guid, title,
342.                     error), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
343.             }
344. 
345.             return true;
346.         }
347. 
348.         // When operations happen on a background thread we have to marshal UI updates back to the UI thread.
349.         private void MarshalLog(string value)
350.         {
351.             // TODO[migrate-001]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
352.             var ignore = this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
353.             {
354.                 Log(value);
355.             });
356.         }
357. 
358.         private void Log(string message)
359.         {
360.             outputField.Text += message + "\r\n";
361.         }
362. 
363.         private void LogStatus(string message, NotifyType type)
364.         {
365.             rootPage.NotifyUser(message, type);
366.             Log(message);
367.         }
368.     }
369. }

29.     /// <summary>
30.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
31.     public sealed partial class Scenario3_Notifications : Page
32.     {
33.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
34.         // as NotifyUser()
35.         private MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
36. 
37.         private BackgroundTransferGroup notificationsGroup;
38. 
39.         private Uri baseUri;
40. 
41.         private static int runId = 0;
42. 
43.         private enum ScenarioType
44.         {
45.             Toast,
46.             Tile
47.         }
48. 
49.         public Scenario3_Notifications()
50.         {
51.             // Use a unique group name so that no other component in the app uses the same group. The recommended way
52.             // is to generate a GUID and use it as group name as shown below.
53.             notificationsGroup = BackgroundTransferGroup.CreateGroup("{296628BF-5AE6-48CE-AA36-86A85A726B6A}");
54. 
55.             // When creating a group, we can optionally define the transfer behavior of transfers associated with the
56.             // group. A "parallel" transfer behavior allows multiple transfers in the same group to run concurrently
57.             // (default). A "serialized" transfer behavior allows at most one default priority transfer at a time for
58.             // the group.
59.             notificationsGroup.TransferBehavior = BackgroundTransferBehavior.Parallel;
60. 
61.             this.InitializeComponent();
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
66.         /// </summary>
67.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
68.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
69.         protected async override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
70.         {
71.             // An application must enumerate downloads when it gets started to prevent stale downloads/uploads.
72.             // Typically this can be done in the App class by overriding OnLaunched() and checking for
73.             // "args.Kind == ActivationKind.Launch" to detect an actual app launch.
74.             // We do it here in the sample to keep the sample code consolidated.
75.             // Note that for this specific scenario we're not interested in downloads from previous instances:
76.             // We'll just enumerate downloads from previous instances and cancel them immediately.
77.             await CancelActiveDownloadsAsync();
78.         }
79. 
80.         private async Task CancelActiveDownloadsAsync()
81.         {
82.             IReadOnlyList<DownloadOperation> downloads = null;
83.             try
84.             {
85.                 // Note that we only enumerate transfers that belong to the transfer group used by this sample
86.                 // scenario. We'll not enumerate transfers started by other sample scenarios in this app.
87.                 downloads = await BackgroundDownloader.GetCurrentDownloadsForTransferGroupAsync(notificationsGroup);
88.             }
89.             catch (Exception ex)
90.             {
91.                 if (!IsExceptionHandled("Discovery error", ex))
92.                 {
93.                     throw;
94.                 }
95.                 return;
96.             }
97. 
98.             // If previous instances of this scenario started transfers that haven't completed yet, cancel them now
99.             // so that we can start this scenario cleanly.
100.             if (downloads.Count > 0)
101.             {
102.                 CancellationTokenSource canceledToken = new CancellationTokenSource();
103.                 canceledToken.Cancel();
104. 
105.                 Task[] tasks = new Task[downloads.Count];
106.                 for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Count; i++)
107.                 {
108.                     tasks[i] = downloads[i].AttachAsync().AsTask(canceledToken.Token);
109.                 }
110. 
111.                 try
112.                 {
113.                     await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
114.                 }
115.                 catch (TaskCanceledException)
116.                 {
117.                 }
118. 
119.                 Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
120.                     "Canceled {0} downloads from previous instances of this scenario.", downloads.Count));
121.             }
122. 
123.             // After cleaning up downloads from previous scenarios, enable buttons to allow the user to run the sample.
124.             ToastNotificationButton.IsEnabled = true;
125.             TileNotificationButton.IsEnabled = true;
126.         }
127. 
128.         private async void ToastNotificationButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
129.         {
130.             // Create a downloader and associate all its downloads with the transfer group used for this scenario.
131.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader();
132.             downloader.TransferGroup = notificationsGroup;
133. 
134.             // Create a ToastNotification that should be shown when all transfers succeed.
135.             // TODO[migrate-007]: see PATTERNS.md#notifications
136.             XmlDocument successToastXml = ToastNotificationManager.GetTemplateContent(ToastTemplateType.ToastText01);
137.             successToastXml.GetElementsByTagName("text").Item(0).InnerText =
138.                 "All three downloads completed successfully.";
139.             ToastNotification successToast = new ToastNotification(successToastXml);
140.             downloader.SuccessToastNotification = successToast;
141. 
142.             // Create a ToastNotification that should be shown if at least one transfer fails.
143.             // TODO[migrate-008]: see PATTERNS.md#notifications
144.             XmlDocument failureToastXml = ToastNotificationManager.GetTemplateContent(ToastTemplateType.ToastText01);
145.             failureToastXml.GetElementsByTagName("text").Item(0).InnerText =
146.                 "At least one download completed with failure.";
147.             ToastNotification failureToast = new ToastNotification(failureToastXml);
148.             downloader.FailureToastNotification = failureToast;
149. 
150.             // Now create and start downloads for the configured BackgroundDownloader object.
151.             await RunDownloadsAsync(downloader, ScenarioType.Toast);
152.         }
153. 
154.         private async void TileNotificationButton_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
155.         {
156.             // Create a downloader and associate all its downloads with the transfer group used for this scenario.
157.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader();
158.             downloader.TransferGroup = notificationsGroup;
159. 
160.             // Create a TileNotification that should be shown when all transfers succeed.
161.             XmlDocument successTileXml = TileUpdateManager.GetTemplateContent(
162.                 TileTemplateType.TileSquare150x150Text03);
163.             XmlNodeList successTextNodes = successTileXml.GetElementsByTagName("text");
164.             successTextNodes.Item(0).InnerText = "All three";
165.             successTextNodes.Item(1).InnerText = "downloads";
166.             successTextNodes.Item(2).InnerText = "completed";
167.             successTextNodes.Item(3).InnerText = "successfully.";
168.             TileNotification successTile = new TileNotification(successTileXml);
169.             successTile.ExpirationTime = DateTime.Now.AddMinutes(10);
170.             downloader.SuccessTileNotification = successTile;
171. 
172.             // Create a TileNotification that should be shown if at least one transfer fails.
173.             XmlDocument failureTileXml = TileUpdateManager.GetTemplateContent(
174.                 TileTemplateType.TileSquare150x150Text03);
175.             XmlNodeList failureTextNodes = failureTileXml.GetElementsByTagName("text");
176.             failureTextNodes.Item(0).InnerText = "At least";
177.             failureTextNodes.Item(1).InnerText = "one download";
178.             failureTextNodes.Item(2).InnerText = "completed";
179.             failureTextNodes.Item(3).InnerText = "with failure.";
180.             TileNotification failureTile = new TileNotification(failureTileXml);
181.             failureTile.ExpirationTime = DateTime.Now.AddMinutes(10);
182.             downloader.FailureTileNotification = failureTile;
183. 
184.             // Now create and start downloads for the configured BackgroundDownloader object.
185.             await RunDownloadsAsync(downloader, ScenarioType.Tile);
186.         }
187. 
188.         private async Task RunDownloadsAsync(BackgroundDownloader downloader, ScenarioType type)
189.         {
190.             // Validating the URI is required since it was received from an untrusted source (user input).
191.             // The URI is validated by calling Uri.TryCreate() that will return 'false' for strings that are not valid URIs.
192.             // Note that when enabling the text box users may provide URIs to machines on the intrAnet that require
193.             // the "Private Networks (Client and Server)" capability.
194.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(serverAddressField.Text.Trim(), UriKind.Absolute, out baseUri))
195.             {
196.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
197.                 return;
198.             }
199. 
200.             // Use a unique ID for every button click, to help the user associate downloads of the same run
201.             // in the logs.
202.             runId++;
203. 
204.             DownloadOperation[] downloads = new DownloadOperation[3];
205. 
206.             try
207.             {
208.                 // First we create three download operations: Note that we don't start downloads immediately. It is
209.                 // important to first create all operations that should participate in the toast/tile update. Once all
210.                 // operations have been created, we can start them.
211.                 // If we start a download and create a second one afterwards, there is a race where the first download
212.                 // may complete before we were able to create the second one. This would result in the toast/tile being
213.                 // shown before we even create the second download.
214.                 downloads[0] = await CreateDownload(downloader, 1, String.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
215.                     "{0}.{1}.FastDownload.txt", type, runId));
216.                 downloads[1] = await CreateDownload(downloader, 5, String.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
217.                     "{0}.{1}.MediumDownload.txt", type, runId));
218.                 downloads[2] = await CreateDownload(downloader, 10, String.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
219.                     "{0}.{1}.SlowDownload.txt", type, runId));
220.             }
221.             catch (FileNotFoundException)
222.             {
223.                 // We were unable to create the destination file.
224.                 return;
225.             }
226. 
227.             // Once all downloads participating in the toast/tile update have been created, start them.
228.             Task[] downloadTasks = new Task[downloads.Length];
229.             for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Length; i++)
230.             {
231.                 downloadTasks[i] = DownloadAsync(downloads[i]);
232.             }
233. 
234.             await Task.WhenAll(downloadTasks);
235.         }
236. 
237.         private async Task<DownloadOperation> CreateDownload(BackgroundDownloader downloader, int delaySeconds,
238.             string fileName)
239.         {
240.             Uri source = new Uri(baseUri, String.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "?delay={0}", delaySeconds));
241. 
242.             StorageFile destinationFile;
243.             try
244.             {
245.                 StorageFolder picturesLibrary = await KnownFolders.GetFolderForUserAsync(null /* current user */, KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
246.                 destinationFile = await picturesLibrary.CreateFileAsync(
247.                     fileName, CreationCollisionOption.GenerateUniqueName);
248.             }
249.             catch (FileNotFoundException ex)
250.             {
251.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Error while creating file: " + ex.Message, NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
252.                 throw;
253.             }
254. 
255.             return downloader.CreateDownload(source, destinationFile);
256.         }
257. 
258.         private async Task DownloadAsync(DownloadOperation download)
259.         {
260.             Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Downloading {0}", download.ResultFile.Name));
261. 
262.             try
263.             {
264.                 await download.StartAsync();
265. 
266.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Downloading {0} completed.", download.ResultFile.Name), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
267.             }
268.             catch (TaskCanceledException)
269.             {
270.             }
271.             catch (Exception ex)
272.             {
273.                 if (!IsExceptionHandled("Execution error", ex, download))
274.                 {
275.                     throw;
276.                 }
277.             }
278.         }
279. 
280.         private bool IsExceptionHandled(string title, Exception ex, DownloadOperation download = null)
281.         {
282.             WebErrorStatus error = BackgroundTransferError.GetStatus(ex.HResult);
283.             if (error == WebErrorStatus.Unknown)
284.             {
285.                 return false;
286.             }
287. 
288.             if (download == null)
289.             {
290.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Error: {0}: {1}", title, error),
291.                     NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
292.             }
293.             else
294.             {
295.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Error: {0} - {1}: {2}", download.ResultFile.Name,
296.                     title, error), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
297.             }
298. 
299.             return true;
300.         }
301. 
302.         private void Log(string message)
303.         {
304.             outputField.Text += message + "\r\n";
305.         }
306. 
307.         private void LogStatus(string message, NotifyType type)
308.         {
309.             rootPage.NotifyUser(message, type);
310.             Log(message);
311.         }
312.     }

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Globalization;
15. using System.Threading;
16. using System.Threading.Tasks;
17. using Windows.Networking.BackgroundTransfer;
18. using Windows.Storage;
19. using Windows.Storage.FileProperties;
20. using Windows.Storage.Pickers;

26. namespace SDKTemplate

28.     /// <summary>
29.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
30.     /// </summary>
31.     public sealed partial class Scenario2_Upload : Page, IDisposable
32.     {
33.         // A pointer back to the main page.  This is needed if you want to call methods in MainPage such
34.         // as NotifyUser()
35.         private MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
36. 
37.         private CancellationTokenSource cts;
38. 
39.         private const int maxUploadFileSize = 100 * 1024 * 1024; // 100 MB (arbitrary limit chosen for this sample)
40. 
41.         public Scenario2_Upload()
42.         {
43.             cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
44. 
45.             this.InitializeComponent();
46.         }
47. 
48.         public void Dispose()
49.         {
50.             if (cts != null)
51.             {
52.                 cts.Dispose();
53.                 cts = null;
54.             }
55. 
56.             GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
57.         }
58. 
59.         /// <summary>
60.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
61.         /// </summary>
62.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
63.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
64.         protected async override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
65.         {
66.             // An application must enumerate uploads when it gets started to prevent stale downloads/uploads.
67.             // Typically this can be done in the App class by overriding OnLaunched() and checking for
68.             // "args.Kind == ActivationKind.Launch" to detect an actual app launch.
69.             // We do it here in the sample to keep the sample code consolidated.
70.             await DiscoverActiveUploadsAsync();
71.         }
72. 
73.         // Enumerate the uploads that were going on in the background while the app was closed.
74.         private async Task DiscoverActiveUploadsAsync()
75.         {
76.             IReadOnlyList<UploadOperation> uploads = null;
77.             try
78.             {
79.                 uploads = await BackgroundUploader.GetCurrentUploadsAsync();
80.             }
81.             catch (Exception ex)
82.             {
83.                 if (!IsExceptionHandled("Discovery error", ex))
84.                 {
85.                     throw;
86.                 }
87.                 return;
88.             }
89. 
90.             Log("Loading background uploads: " + uploads.Count);
91. 
92.             if (uploads.Count > 0)
93.             {
94.                 List<Task> tasks = new List<Task>();
95.                 foreach (UploadOperation upload in uploads)
96.                 {
97.                     Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Discovered background upload: {0}, Status: {1}", 
98.                         upload.Guid, upload.Progress.Status));
99. 
100.                     // Attach progress and completion handlers.
101.                     tasks.Add(HandleUploadAsync(upload, false));
102.                 }
103. 
104.                 // Don't await HandleUploadAsync() in the foreach loop since we would attach to the second
105.                 // upload only when the first one completed; attach to the third upload when the second one
106.                 // completes etc. We want to attach to all uploads immediately.
107.                 // If there are actions that need to be taken once uploads complete, await tasks here, outside
108.                 // the loop.
109.                 await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
110.             }
111.         }
112. 
113.         private void StartUpload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
114.         {
115.             // Validating the URI is required since it was received from an untrusted source (user input).
116.             // The URI is validated by calling Uri.TryCreate() that will return 'false' for strings that are not valid URIs.
117.             // Note that when enabling the text box users may provide URIs to machines on the intrAnet that require
118.             // the "Private Networks (Client and Server)" capability.
119.             Uri uri;
120.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(serverAddressField.Text.Trim(), UriKind.Absolute, out uri))
121.             {
122.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
123.                 return;
124.             }
125. 
126.             // TODO[migrate-002]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
127.             FileOpenPicker picker = new FileOpenPicker();
128.             picker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");
129. 
130.             StartSingleFileUpload(picker, uri);
131.         }
132. 
133.         private async void UploadSingleFile(Uri uri, StorageFile file)
134.         {
135.             if (file == null)
136.             {
137.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("No file selected.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
138.                 return;
139.             }
140. 
141.             BasicProperties properties = await file.GetBasicPropertiesAsync();
142.             if (properties.Size > maxUploadFileSize)
143.             {
144.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, 
145.                     "Selected file exceeds max. upload file size ({0} MB).", maxUploadFileSize / (1024 * 1024)),
146.                     NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
147.                 return;
148.             }
149. 
150.             BackgroundUploader uploader = new BackgroundUploader();
151.             uploader.SetRequestHeader("Filename", file.Name);
152. 
153.             UploadOperation upload = uploader.CreateUpload(uri, file);
154.             Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Uploading {0} to {1}, {2}", file.Name, uri.AbsoluteUri, 
155.                 upload.Guid));
156. 
157.             // Attach progress and completion handlers.
158.             await HandleUploadAsync(upload, true);
159.         }
160. 
161.         private void StartMultipartUpload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
162.         {
163.             // By default 'serverAddressField' is disabled and URI validation is not required. When enabling the text
164.             // box validating the URI is required since it was received from an untrusted source (user input).
165.             // The URI is validated by calling Uri.TryCreate() that will return 'false' for strings that are not valid URIs.
166.             // Note that when enabling the text box users may provide URIs to machines on the intrAnet that require
167.             // the "Private Networks (Client and Server)" capability.
168.             Uri uri;
169.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(serverAddressField.Text.Trim(), UriKind.Absolute, out uri))
170.             {
171.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
172.                 return;
173.             }
174. 
175.             // TODO[migrate-003]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
176.             FileOpenPicker picker = new FileOpenPicker();
177.             picker.FileTypeFilter.Add("*");
178. 
179.             StartMultipleFilesUpload(picker, uri);
180.         }
181. 
182.         // TODO[migrate-004]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
183.         private async void StartSingleFileUpload(FileOpenPicker picker, Uri uri)
184.         {
185.             StorageFile file = await picker.PickSingleFileAsync();
186.             UploadSingleFile(uri, file);
187.         }
188. 
189.         // TODO[migrate-005]: see PATTERNS.md#pickers
190.         private async void StartMultipleFilesUpload(FileOpenPicker picker, Uri uri)
191.         {
192.             IReadOnlyList<StorageFile> files = await picker.PickMultipleFilesAsync();
193.             UploadMultipleFiles(uri, files);
194.         }
195. 
196.         private async void UploadMultipleFiles(Uri uri, IReadOnlyList<StorageFile> files)
197.         {
198.             if (files.Count == 0)
199.             {
200.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("No file selected.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
201.                 return;
202.             }
203. 
204.             ulong totalFileSize = 0;
205.             for (int i = 0; i < files.Count; i++)
206.             {
207.                 BasicProperties properties = await files[i].GetBasicPropertiesAsync();
208.                 totalFileSize += properties.Size;
209. 
210.                 if (totalFileSize > maxUploadFileSize)
211.                 {
212.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture,
213.                         "Size of selected files exceeds max. upload file size ({0} MB).", 
214.                         maxUploadFileSize / (1024 * 1024)), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
215.                     return;
216.                 }
217.             }
218. 
219.             List<BackgroundTransferContentPart> parts = new List<BackgroundTransferContentPart>();
220.             for (int i = 0; i < files.Count; i++)
221.             {
222.                 BackgroundTransferContentPart part = new BackgroundTransferContentPart("File" + i, files[i].Name);
223.                 part.SetFile(files[i]);
224.                 parts.Add(part);
225.             }
226. 
227.             BackgroundUploader uploader = new BackgroundUploader();
228.             UploadOperation upload = await uploader.CreateUploadAsync(uri, parts);
229. 
230.             String fileNames = files[0].Name;
231.             for (int i = 1; i < files.Count; i++)
232.             {
233.                 fileNames += ", " + files[i].Name;
234.             }
235. 
236.             Log(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Uploading {0} to {1}, {2}", fileNames, uri.AbsoluteUri, 
237.                 upload.Guid));
238. 
239.             // Attach progress and completion handlers.
240.             await HandleUploadAsync(upload, true);
241.         }
242. 
243.         private void CancelAll_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
244.         {
245.             Log("Canceling all active uploads");
246. 
247.             cts.Cancel();
248.             cts.Dispose();
249. 
250.             // Re-create the CancellationTokenSource and activeUploads for future uploads.
251.             cts = new CancellationTokenSource();
252.         }
253. 
254.         // Note that this event is invoked on a background thread, so we cannot access the UI directly.
255.         private void UploadProgress(UploadOperation upload)
256.         {
257.             // UploadOperation.Progress is updated in real-time while the operation is ongoing. Therefore,
258.             // we must make a local copy so that we can have a consistent view of that ever-changing state
259.             // throughout this method's lifetime.
260.             BackgroundUploadProgress currentProgress = upload.Progress;
261. 
262.             MarshalLog(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Progress: {0}, Status: {1}", upload.Guid, 
263.                 currentProgress.Status));
264. 
265.             double percentSent = 100;
266.             if (currentProgress.TotalBytesToSend > 0)
267.             {
268.                 percentSent = currentProgress.BytesSent * 100 / currentProgress.TotalBytesToSend;
269.             }
270. 
271.             MarshalLog(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, 
272.                 " - Sent bytes: {0} of {1} ({2}%), Received bytes: {3} of {4}", currentProgress.BytesSent, 
273.                 currentProgress.TotalBytesToSend, percentSent, currentProgress.BytesReceived, currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive));
274. 
275.             if (currentProgress.HasRestarted)
276.             {
277.                 MarshalLog(" - Upload restarted");
278.             }
279. 
280.             if (currentProgress.HasResponseChanged)
281.             {
282.                 // We've received new response headers from the server.
283.                 MarshalLog(" - Response updated; Header count: " + upload.GetResponseInformation().Headers.Count);
284. 
285.                 // If you want to stream the response data this is a good time to start.
286.                 // upload.GetResultStreamAt(0);
287.             }
288.         }
289. 
290.         private async Task HandleUploadAsync(UploadOperation upload, bool start)
291.         {
292.             try
293.             {
294.                 LogStatus("Running: " + upload.Guid, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
295. 
296.                 Progress<UploadOperation> progressCallback = new Progress<UploadOperation>(UploadProgress);
297.                 if (start)
298.                 {
299.                     // Start the upload and attach a progress handler.
300.                     await upload.StartAsync().AsTask(cts.Token, progressCallback);
301.                 }
302.                 else
303.                 {
304.                     // The upload was already running when the application started, re-attach the progress handler.
305.                     await upload.AttachAsync().AsTask(cts.Token, progressCallback);
306.                 }
307. 
308.                 ResponseInformation response = upload.GetResponseInformation();
309. 
310.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Completed: {0}, Status Code: {1}", upload.Guid,
311.                     response.StatusCode), NotifyType.StatusMessage);
312.             }
313.             catch (TaskCanceledException)
314.             {
315.                 LogStatus("Canceled: " + upload.Guid, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
316.             }
317.             catch (Exception ex)
318.             {
319.                 if (!IsExceptionHandled("Error", ex, upload))
320.                 {
321.                     throw;
322.                 }
323.             }
324.         }
325. 
326.         private bool IsExceptionHandled(string title, Exception ex, UploadOperation upload = null)
327.         {
328.             WebErrorStatus error = BackgroundTransferError.GetStatus(ex.HResult);
329.             if (error == WebErrorStatus.Unknown)
330.             {
331.                 return false;
332.             }
333. 
334.             if (upload == null)
335.             {
336.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Error: {0}: {1}", title, error), 
337.                     NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
338.             }
339.             else
340.             {
341.                 LogStatus(String.Format(CultureInfo.CurrentCulture, "Error: {0} - {1}: {2}", upload.Guid, title, 
342.                     error), NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
343.             }
344. 
345.             return true;
346.         }
347. 
348.         // When operations happen on a background thread we have to marshal UI updates back to the UI thread.
349.         private void MarshalLog(string value)
350.         {
351.             // TODO[migrate-006]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
352.             var ignore = this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
353.             {
354.                 Log(value);
355.             });
356.         }
357. 
358.         private void Log(string message)
359.         {
360.             outputField.Text += message + "\r\n";
361.         }
362. 
363.         private void LogStatus(string message, NotifyType type)
364.         {
365.             rootPage.NotifyUser(message, type);
366.             Log(message);
367.         }
368.     }
369. }

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Threading;
15. using System.Threading.Tasks;
16. using Windows.Foundation;
17. using Windows.Networking.BackgroundTransfer;
18. using Windows.Storage;
19. using Windows.Storage.Streams;
