27. {
28.     public sealed partial class Scenario5_RandomAccess : Page
29.     {
30.         private MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
31. 
32.         // Constants.
33.         private const uint BytesPerMegaByte = 1000000;
34. 
35.         // Scenario instance state.
36.         private DownloadOperation download = null;
37.         private IRandomAccessStreamWithContentType randomAccessStream = null;
38.         private Task<IBuffer> readOperation = null;
39.         private CancellationTokenSource readCancellationTokenSource = new CancellationTokenSource();
40. 
41.         public Scenario5_RandomAccess()
42.         {
43.             InitializeComponent();
44.         }
45. 
46.         protected async override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
47.         {
48.             // An application must enumerate downloads when it gets started to prevent stale downloads/uploads.
49.             // Typically this can be done in the App class by overriding OnLaunched() and checking for
50.             // "args.Kind == ActivationKind.Launch" to detect an actual app launch.
51.             // We do it here in the sample to keep the sample code consolidated.
52.             // Note that for this specific scenario we are not interested in downloads from previous instances, so
53.             // we just enumerate all downloads from previous instances and cancel them immediately.
54.             await CancelActiveDownloadsAsync();
55.         }
56. 
57.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
58.         {
59.             // Cancel any outstanding reads.
60.             readCancellationTokenSource.Cancel();
61.         }
62. 
63.         private async Task CancelActiveDownloadsAsync()
64.         {
65.             IReadOnlyList<DownloadOperation> downloads;
66.             try
67.             {
68.                 downloads = await BackgroundDownloader.GetCurrentDownloadsAsync();
69.             }
70.             catch (Exception ex) when (IsWebException("Discovery error", ex))
71.             {
72.                 // Don't worry if the old downloads encountered web exceptions.
73.                 return;
74.             }
75. 
76.             // If previous sample instances/scenarios started transfers that haven't completed yet, cancel them now
77.             // so that we can start this scenario cleanly.
78.             if (downloads.Count > 0)
79.             {
80.                 using (CancellationTokenSource canceledToken = new CancellationTokenSource())
81.                 {
82.                     canceledToken.Cancel();
83. 
84.                     Task[] tasks = new Task[downloads.Count];
85.                     for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Count; i++)
86.                     {
87.                         tasks[i] = downloads[i].AttachAsync().AsTask(canceledToken.Token);
88.                     }
89. 
90.                     try
91.                     {
92.                         await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
93.                     }
94.                     catch (TaskCanceledException)
95.                     {
96.                     }
97.                 }
98.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"Canceled {downloads.Count} downloads.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
99.             }
100.         }
101. 
102.         private void PauseDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
103.         {
104.             if (download != null)
105.             {
106.                 download.Pause();
107.                 pauseDownloadButton.IsEnabled = false;
108.                 resumeDownloadButton.IsEnabled = true;
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void ResumeDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
113.         {
114.             if (download != null)
115.             {
116.                 download.Resume();
117.                 pauseDownloadButton.IsEnabled = true;
118.                 resumeDownloadButton.IsEnabled = false;
119.             }
120.         }
121. 
122.         private void SeekDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
123.         {
124.             // Cancel the ongoing read operation (if any).
125.             if (readOperation != null)
126.             {
127.                 readCancellationTokenSource.Cancel();
128. 
129.                 // Re-create the CancellationTokenSource for future reads.
130.                 readCancellationTokenSource = new CancellationTokenSource();
131.             }
132. 
133.             if (randomAccessStream != null)
134.             {
135.                 // Update the stream's seek position to the user-selected one.
136.                 // The new seek position will take effect on the next ReadAsync operation.
137.                 var seekPositionInMegaBytes = (ulong)seekSlider.Value;
138.                 ulong seekPositionInBytes = seekPositionInMegaBytes * BytesPerMegaByte;
139.                 randomAccessStream.Seek(seekPositionInBytes);
140.             }
141.         }
142. 
143.         private static string FormatDownloadedRanges(IEnumerable<BackgroundTransferFileRange> ranges)
144.         {
145.             string result = "";
146.             foreach (BackgroundTransferFileRange range in ranges)
147.             {
148.                 if (result != "")
149.                 {
150.                     result += ", ";
151.                 }
152. 
153.                 result += $"{range.Offset}-{range.Offset + range.Length - 1}";
154.             }
155. 
156.             if (result == "")
157.             {
158.                 result = "None";
159.             }
160.             return result;
161.         }
162. 
163.         private async void OnDownloadProgress(IAsyncOperationWithProgress<DownloadOperation, DownloadOperation> sender, DownloadOperation progress)
164.         {
165.             // We capture a snapshot of DownloadOperation.Progress because
166.             // it is is updated in real-time while the operation is ongoing.
167.             BackgroundDownloadProgress currentProgress = progress.Progress;
168. 
169.             // TODO[migrate-011]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
170.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
171.             {
172.                 // Prepare the progress message to display in the UI.
173.                 ulong percent = 100;
174.                 if (currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive > 0)
175.                 {
176.                     percent = currentProgress.BytesReceived * 100 / currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive;
177.                 }
178. 
179.                 DownloadedStatusText.Text = $"{currentProgress.Status} - {percent}% downloaded.";
180.             });
181.         }
182. 
183.         private async void OnRangesDownloaded(DownloadOperation sender, BackgroundTransferRangesDownloadedEventArgs args)
184.         {
185.             // BackgroundTransfer will wait for the app's RangesDownloaded event handlers to finish executing before
186.             // triggering the next RangesDownloaded event. Any download progress made while the app's handlers are
187.             // executing get coalesced into a single RangesDownloaded event.
188.             //
189.             // When calling async APIs inside this handler, we must first take a deferral and then complete it once we
190.             // are done processing the event. Otherwise, BackgroundTransfer will assume that the handler is done executing
191.             // as soon as the synchronous portion of the handler returns, even if there are still tasks running.
192.             using (args.GetDeferral())
193.             {
194.                 // AddedRanges tells you which ranges were downloaded since the last RangesDownloaded event.
195.                 // To get the full list of downloaded ranges, use the GetDownloadedRanges method.
196.                 string message = " Newly-downloaded ranges: " + FormatDownloadedRanges(args.AddedRanges);
197. 
198.                 // A download may restart if the server contents changed while the download is in progress.
199.                 if (args.WasDownloadRestarted)
200.                 {
201.                     message += " (download was restarted)";
202.                 }
203. 
204.                 // Wait for the UI update to happen before completing the deferral. That way, we can be certain that
205.                 // the next RangesDownloaded event will not get triggered midway through the UI update.
206.                 // TODO[migrate-012]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
207.                 await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
208.                 {
209.                     DownloadedRangesText.Text = FormatDownloadedRanges(sender.GetDownloadedRanges());
210.                     rootPage.NotifyUser(message, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
211.                 });
212.             }
213.         }
214. 
215.         private async void StartDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
216.         {
217.             // Reset the output whenever a new download attempt begins.
218.             DownloadedInfoText.Text = "";
219.             DownloadedStatusText.Text = "";
220.             DownloadedRangesText.Text = "None";
221.             PreviousReadText.Text = "";
222.             CurrentReadText.Text = "";
223. 
224.             Uri source;
225.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(serverAddressField.Text.Trim(), UriKind.Absolute, out source))
226.             {
227.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
228.                 return;
229.             }
230. 
231.             string destination = fileNameField.Text.Trim();
232.             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destination))
233.             {
234.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("A local file name is required.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
235.                 return;
236.             }
237. 
238.             StorageFolder picturesLibrary = await KnownFolders.GetFolderForUserAsync(null /* current user */, KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
239.             StorageFile destinationFile;
240.             try
241.             {
242.                 destinationFile = await picturesLibrary.CreateFileAsync(destination, CreationCollisionOption.GenerateUniqueName);
243.             }
244.             catch (Exception ex)
245.             {
246.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Error while creating file: " + ex.Message, NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
247.                 return;
248.             }
249. 
250.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader();
251.             download = downloader.CreateDownload(source, destinationFile);
252. 
253.             // Apps need to set the IsRandomAccessRequired boolean property to TRUE (default is FALSE) before they
254.             // call StartAsync() on a DownloadOperation object if they want to use the random access feature. After
255.             // opting into "random access" mode, GetResultRandomAccessStreamReference() can be called.
256.             //
257.             // Setting IsRandomAccessRequired to TRUE before starting the download is necessary because the
258.             // server-side requirements for Random Access functionality are stricter than for regular (sequential)
259.             // downloads, so BackgroundTransfer needs to know the app's intentions in advance. That way, it can fail
260.             // the transfer up front if the server does not satisfy those additional requirements (e.g., HTTP range
261.             // header support).
262.             download.IsRandomAccessRequired = true;
263. 
264.             // Subscribe to RangesDownloaded notifications.
265.             download.RangesDownloaded += OnRangesDownloaded;
266. 
267.             DownloadedInfoText.Text = $"Downloading to {destinationFile.Name}, {download.Guid}";
268. 
269.             // Start the download and read partially-downloaded content while the transfer is still ongoing.
270.             await StartDownloadAndReadContentsAsync();
271.         }
272. 
273.         private async Task StartDownloadAndReadContentsAsync()
274.         {
275.             try
276.             {
277.                 // Retrieve a random access stream from the download operation. Every OpenReadAsync() operation returns
278.                 // a new stream instance that is independent of previous ones (i.e., the seek position of one stream
279.                 // isn't affected by calls on another stream).
280.                 //
281.                 // This sample demonstrates the direct usage of a DownloadOperation's random access stream and its
282.                 // effects on the ongoing transfer. However, bear in mind that there are a variety of operations
283.                 // that can manage the stream on the app's behalf for specific scenarios. For instance, a
284.                 // DownloadOperation pointing to a video URL can be consumed by the MediaPlayer class in four easy
285.                 // steps:
286.                 //
287.                 // var randomAccessStreamReference = download.GetResultRandomAccessStreamReference();
288.                 // stream = await randomAccessStreamReference.OpenReadAsync();
289.                 // var mediaPlayer = new Windows.Media.Playback.MediaPlayer();
290.                 // mediaPlayer.Source = Windows.Media.Core.MediaSource.CreateFromStream(stream, stream.ContentType);
291. 
292.                 var randomAccessStreamReference = download.GetResultRandomAccessStreamReference();
293.                 randomAccessStream = await randomAccessStreamReference.OpenReadAsync();
294. 
295.                 // Start the download. If the server doesn't support random access, the download will fail
296.                 // with WebErrorStatus.InsufficientRangeSupport or WebErrorStatus.MissingContentLengthSupport.
297.                 IAsyncOperationWithProgress<DownloadOperation, DownloadOperation> downloadOperation = download.StartAsync();
298.                 downloadOperation.Progress += OnDownloadProgress;
299.                 Task downloadTask = downloadOperation.AsTask();
300. 
301.                 startDownloadButton.IsEnabled = false;
302.                 pauseDownloadButton.IsEnabled = true;
303. 
304.                 // Read data while the download is still ongoing. Use a 1 MB read buffer for that purpose.
305.                 var readBuffer = new Windows.Storage.Streams.Buffer(BytesPerMegaByte);
306.                 while (!downloadTask.IsCompleted)
307.                 {
308.                     ulong readOffsetInBytes = randomAccessStream.Position;
309. 
310.                     PreviousReadText.Text = CurrentReadText.Text;
311.                     CurrentReadText.Text = $"Reading from offset {readOffsetInBytes:n0}";
312. 
313.                     readOperation = randomAccessStream.ReadAsync(
314.                         readBuffer,
315.                         readBuffer.Capacity,
316.                         InputStreamOptions.None).
317.                         AsTask(readCancellationTokenSource.Token);
318. 
319.                     // Update the UI to show the current read's position.
320.                     currentPositionSlider.Value = readOffsetInBytes / BytesPerMegaByte;
321. 
322.                     try
323.                     {
324.                         // Wait for the read to complete.
325.                         IBuffer bytesRead = await readOperation;
326.                         CurrentReadText.Text += $", completed with {bytesRead.Length:n0} bytes";
327. 
328.                         // At this point, a real app would process the 'bytesRead' data to do something interesting
329.                         // with it (e.g., display video and/or play audio).
330. 
331.                         if (randomAccessStream.Position >= randomAccessStream.Size)
332.                         {
333.                             // We have reached EOF. Wrap around to the beginning while we wait for the download to complete.
334.                             randomAccessStream.Seek(0);
335.                         }
336.                     }
337.                     catch (OperationCanceledException)
338.                     {
339.                         // The ongoing read was canceled by SeekDownload_Click(...) in order for a new download
340.                         // position to take effect immediately.
341.                         CurrentReadText.Text += ", cancelled.";
342.                     }
343.                 }
344. 
345.                 // Wait for the download to complete.
346.                 await downloadTask;
347. 
348.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Download completed successfully", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
349.             }
350.             catch (Exception ex) when (IsWebException("Execution error", ex))
351.             {
352.                 // Abandon the operation if a web exception occurs.
353.             }
354.             finally
355.             {
356.                 download = null;
357.                 randomAccessStream = null;
358.                 readOperation = null;
359. 
360.                 startDownloadButton.IsEnabled = true;
361.                 pauseDownloadButton.IsEnabled = false;
362.                 resumeDownloadButton.IsEnabled = false;
363.             }
364.         }
365. 
366.         private bool IsWebException(string title, Exception ex)
367.         {
368.             WebErrorStatus error = BackgroundTransferError.GetStatus(ex.HResult);
369.             bool result = (error != WebErrorStatus.Unknown);
370.             string message = result ? error.ToString() : ex.Message;
371.             if (download == null)
372.             {
373.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
374.             }
375.             else
376.             {
377.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{download.Guid} - {title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
378.             }
379. 
380.             return result;
381.         }

27.     public sealed partial class Scenario6_RecoverableErrors : Page
28.     {
29.         private MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
30. 
31.         // Scenario instance state.
32.         private DownloadOperation download = null;
33.         private CancellationTokenSource downloadCancellationTokenSource = new CancellationTokenSource();
34. 
35.         public Scenario6_RecoverableErrors()
36.         {
37.             InitializeComponent();
38.         }
39. 
40.         /// <summary>
41.         /// Invoked when this page is about to be displayed in a Frame.
42.         /// </summary>
43.         /// <param name="e">Event data that describes how this page was reached.  The Parameter
44.         /// property is typically used to configure the page.</param>
45.         protected async override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
46.         {
47.             // An application must enumerate downloads when it gets started to prevent stale downloads/uploads.
48.             // Typically this can be done in the App class by overriding OnLaunched() and checking for
49.             // "args.Kind == ActivationKind.Launch" to detect an actual app launch.
50.             // We do it here in the sample to keep the sample code consolidated.
51.             // Note that for this specific scenario we are not interested in downloads from previous instances, so
52.             // we just enumerate all downloads from previous instances and cancel them immediately.
53.             await CancelActiveDownloadsAsync();
54.         }
55. 
56.         protected override void OnNavigatedFrom(NavigationEventArgs e)
57.         {
58.             downloadCancellationTokenSource.Cancel();
59.         }
60. 
61.         private async Task CancelActiveDownloadsAsync()
62.         {
63.             IReadOnlyList<DownloadOperation> downloads;
64.             try
65.             {
66.                 downloads = await BackgroundDownloader.GetCurrentDownloadsAsync();
67.             }
68.             catch (Exception ex) when (IsWebException("Discovery error", ex))
69.             {
70.                 // Don't worry if the old downloads encountered web exceptions.
71.                 return;
72.             }
73. 
74.             // If previous sample instances/scenarios started transfers that haven't completed yet, cancel them now
75.             // so that we can start this scenario cleanly.
76.             if (downloads.Count > 0)
77.             {
78.                 using (CancellationTokenSource canceledToken = new CancellationTokenSource())
79.                 {
80.                     canceledToken.Cancel();
81. 
82.                     Task[] tasks = new Task[downloads.Count];
83.                     for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Count; i++)
84.                     {
85.                         tasks[i] = downloads[i].AttachAsync().AsTask(canceledToken.Token);
86.                     }
87. 
88.                     try
89.                     {
90.                         await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
91.                     }
92.                     catch (TaskCanceledException)
93.                     {
94.                     }
95.                 }
96. 
97.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"Canceled {downloads.Count} downloads.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
98.             }
99.         }
100. 
101.         private async void OnDownloadProgress(IAsyncOperationWithProgress<DownloadOperation, DownloadOperation> sender, DownloadOperation progress)
102.         {
103.             // We capture a snapshot of DownloadOperation.Progress because
104.             // it is is updated in real-time while the operation is ongoing.
105.             BackgroundDownloadProgress currentProgress = progress.Progress;
106. 
107.             // TODO[migrate-013]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
108.             await Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, async () =>
109.             {
110.                 // Prepare the progress message to display in the UI.
111.                 ulong percent = 100;
112.                 if (currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive > 0)
113.                 {
114.                     percent = currentProgress.BytesReceived * 100 / currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive;
115.                 }
116. 
117.                 DownloadedStatusText.Text = $"{currentProgress.Status} - {percent}% downloaded.";
118. 
119.                 if (currentProgress.HasRestarted)
120.                 {
121.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("Download has restarted.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
122.                 }
123. 
124.                 if (currentProgress.Status == BackgroundTransferStatus.PausedRecoverableWebErrorStatus)
125.                 {
126.                     // The only value we put in RecoverableWebErrorStatuses is WebErrorStatus.Forbidden,
127.                     // so that will be the only value observed here.
128.                     System.Diagnostics.Debug.Assert(download.CurrentWebErrorStatus == WebErrorStatus.Forbidden);
129. 
130.                     rootPage.NotifyUser("URL has expired.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
131. 
132.                     // The URL expired. Ask the user for information so we can get a new URL.
133.                     ContentDialogResult result = await ReauthorizeDialog.ShowAsync();
134. 
135.                     if (result == ContentDialogResult.Primary)
136.                     {
137.                         // For the purpose of this sample, we simply remove "?shouldExpire=yes" from the URL
138.                         // to indicate that the sample server should treat it like a new, unexpired URL.
139.                         string originalUrlString = download.RequestedUri.OriginalString;
140.                         string newUrlString = originalUrlString.Replace("?shouldExpire=yes", "");
141. 
142.                         rootPage.NotifyUser("Updating URL and resuming the download.", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
143.                         download.RequestedUri = new Uri(newUrlString);
144.                         download.Resume();
145.                     }
146.                     else
147.                     {
148.                         // Cancel the download.
149.                         downloadCancellationTokenSource.Cancel();
150.                         downloadCancellationTokenSource.Dispose();
151. 
152.                         // Re-create the CancellationTokenSource for future downloads.
153.                         downloadCancellationTokenSource = new CancellationTokenSource();
154.                     }
155.                 }
156.             });
157.         }
158. 
159.         private async void StartDownload_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
160.         {
161.             // Reset the output whenever a new download attempt begins.
162.             DownloadedInfoText.Text = "";
163.             DownloadedStatusText.Text = "";
164.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
165. 
166.             Uri source;
167.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(serverAddressField.Text.Trim(), UriKind.Absolute, out source))
168.             {
169.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
170.                 return;
171.             }
172. 
173.             string destination = fileNameField.Text.Trim();
174.             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(destination))
175.             {
176.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("A local file name is required.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
177.                 return;
178.             }
179. 
180.             StorageFolder picturesLibrary = await KnownFolders.GetFolderForUserAsync(null /* current user */, KnownFolderId.PicturesLibrary);
181.             StorageFile destinationFile;
182.             try
183.             {
184.                 destinationFile = await picturesLibrary.CreateFileAsync(destination, CreationCollisionOption.GenerateUniqueName);
185.             }
186.             catch (Exception ex)
187.             {
188.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"Error while creating file: {ex.Message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
189.                 return;
190.             }
191. 
192.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader();
193.             download = downloader.CreateDownload(source, destinationFile);
194. 
195.             // Opt into "random access" mode. Transfers configured this way have full support for resumable URL updates.
196.             // If the timestamp or file size of the updated URL is different from that of the previous URL, the download
197.             // will restart from scratch. Otherwise, the transfer will resume from the same position using the new URL.
198.             //
199.             // Due to OS limitations, downloads that don't opt into "random access" mode will always restart from scratch
200.             // whenever their URL is updated.
201.             download.IsRandomAccessRequired = true;
202. 
203.             if (configureRecoverableErrorsCheckBox.IsChecked.Value)
204.             {
205.                 // Declare HTTP 403 (WebErrorStatus.Forbidden) as a recoverable error.
206.                 download.RecoverableWebErrorStatuses.Add(WebErrorStatus.Forbidden);
207.             }
208. 
209.             DownloadedInfoText.Text = $"Downloading to {destinationFile.Name}, {download.Guid}";
210. 
211.             // Start the download and wait for it to complete.
212.             await HandleDownloadAsync();
213.         }
214. 
215.         private async Task HandleDownloadAsync()
216.         {
217.             try
218.             {
219.                 startDownloadButton.IsEnabled = false;
220.                 IAsyncOperationWithProgress<DownloadOperation, DownloadOperation> downloadOperation = download.StartAsync();
221.                 downloadOperation.Progress += OnDownloadProgress;
222.                 await downloadOperation.AsTask(downloadCancellationTokenSource.Token);
223. 
224.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Download completed successfully", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
225.             }
226.             catch (Exception ex) when (IsWebException("Execution error", ex))
227.             {
228.             }
229.             catch (TaskCanceledException)
230.             {
231.                 // Download was canceled.
232.             }
233.             finally
234.             {
235.                 download = null;
236.                 startDownloadButton.IsEnabled = true;
237.             }
238.         }
239. 
240.         private bool IsWebException(string title, Exception ex)
241.         {
242.             WebErrorStatus error = BackgroundTransferError.GetStatus(ex.HResult);
243.             bool result = (error != WebErrorStatus.Unknown);
244.             string message = result ? error.ToString() : ex.Message;
245.             if (download == null)
246.             {
247.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
248.             }
249.             else
250.             {
251.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{download.Guid} - {title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
252.             }
253. 
254.             return result;
255.         }

96.             new ObservableCollection<DownloadOperationItem>();

99.         {
100.             reorderGroup = BackgroundTransferGroup.CreateGroup("{7421B969-18D4-4532-B6BD-22BDABF71C08}");
101.             reorderGroup.TransferBehavior = BackgroundTransferBehavior.Serialized;

112.         private async Task CancelActiveDownloadsAsync()
113.         {
114.             // Only the downloads that belong to the transfer group used by this sample scenario
115.             // will be canceled.
116.             IReadOnlyList<DownloadOperation> downloads = null;
117.             try
118.             {
119.                 downloads = await BackgroundDownloader.GetCurrentDownloadsForTransferGroupAsync(reorderGroup);
120.             }
121.             catch (Exception ex)
122.             {
123.                 if (!IsWebException("Discovery error", ex))
124.                 {
125.                     throw;
126.                 }
127.                 return;
128.             }
129. 
130.             // If previous instances of this scenario started transfers that haven't completed yet,
131.             // cancel them now so that we can start this scenario cleanly.
132.             if (downloads.Count > 0)
133.             {
134.                 CancellationTokenSource cancellationToken = new CancellationTokenSource();
135.                 cancellationToken.Cancel();
136. 
137.                 Task[] tasks = new Task[downloads.Count];
138.                 for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Count; i++)
139.                 {
140.                     tasks[i] = downloads[i].AttachAsync().AsTask(cancellationToken.Token);
141.                 }
142. 
143.                 // Cancel each download and ignore the cancellation exception.
144.                 try
145.                 {
146.                     await Task.WhenAll(tasks);
147.                 }
148.                 catch (TaskCanceledException)
149.                 {
150.                 }
151.             }

155.         {

157.

160. 
161.             // After all downloads are complete, let the user start new ones again.

174.         private async Task RunDownloadsAsync()
175.         {
176.             // Create a downloader and associate all its downloads with the transfer group used for
177.             // this scenario.
178.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader();
179.             downloader.TransferGroup = reorderGroup;
180. 
181.             // Validating the URI is required since it was received from an untrusted source (user
182.             // input).
183.             // The URI is validated by calling Uri.TryCreate() that will return 'false' for strings
184.             // that are not valid URIs.
185.             // Note that when enabling the text box users may provide URIs to machines on the
186.             // intranet that require the "Private Networks (Client and Server)" capability.
187.             string remoteAddress = remoteAddressField.Text.Trim();
188.             Uri tmpUri;
189.             if (!Uri.TryCreate(remoteAddress, UriKind.Absolute, out tmpUri))
190.             {
191.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid URI.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
192.                 return;
193.             }
194. 
195.             string fileName = fileNameField.Text.Trim();
196.             if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(fileName))
197.             {
198.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("A local file name is required.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
199.                 return;
200.             }
201. 
202.             // Try to create some downloads.
203.             DownloadOperation[] downloads = new DownloadOperation[NumDownloads];
204.             try
205.             {
206.                 for (int i = 0; i < NumDownloads; i++)
207.                 {
208.                     string currRemoteAddress = $"{remoteAddress}?id={i}";
209.                     string currFileName = $"{i}.{fileName}";
210.                     downloads[i] = await CreateDownload(downloader, currRemoteAddress, currFileName);
211.                 }
212.             }
213.             catch (FileNotFoundException ex)
214.             {
215.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"Error while creating file: {ex.Message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
216.                 return;
217.             }
218. 
219.             // Once all downloads have been created, start them.
220.             Task[] downloadTasks = new Task[downloads.Length];
221.             downloadCollection.Clear();
222.             for (int i = 0; i < downloads.Length; i++)
223.             {
224.                 DownloadOperationItem item = new DownloadOperationItem(downloads[i]);
225.                 downloadTasks[i] = DownloadAsync(item);
226.                 downloadCollection.Add(item);
227.             }
228. 
229.             await Task.WhenAll(downloadTasks);
230.         }
231. 
232.         private async Task<DownloadOperation> CreateDownload(
233.             BackgroundDownloader downloader,
234.             string remoteAddress,
235.             string fileName)
236.         {
237.             Uri source = new Uri(remoteAddress);
238. 
239.             StorageFile destinationFile;
240.             try
241.             {
242.                 destinationFile = await KnownFolders.PicturesLibrary.CreateFileAsync(
243.                     fileName,
244.                     CreationCollisionOption.GenerateUniqueName);
245.             }
246.             catch (FileNotFoundException ex)
247.             {
248.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"Error while creating file: {ex.Message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
249.                 throw;
250.             }
251. 
252.             return downloader.CreateDownload(source, destinationFile);
253.         }
254. 
255.         private async Task DownloadAsync(DownloadOperationItem item)
256.         {
257.             Progress<DownloadOperation> progressCallback = new Progress<DownloadOperation>(
258.                 (operation) => DownloadProgress(item));
259. 
260.             try
261.             {
262.                 await item.download.StartAsync().AsTask(progressCallback);
263. 
264.                 item.stateText = item.download.Progress.Status.ToString();
265.                 rootPage.NotifyUser(
266.                     $"Downloading {item.download.ResultFile.Name} completed.",
267.                     NotifyType.StatusMessage);
268.             }
269.             catch (Exception ex)
270.             {
271.                 // Ignore canceled downloads since they are not displayed.
272.                 if (item.download.Progress.Status != BackgroundTransferStatus.Canceled)
273.                 {
274.                     // Ensure that we reach 100% even for errors.
275.                     item.percentComplete = 100;
276.                     item.stateText = item.download.Progress.Status.ToString();
277.                     if (!IsWebException("Execution error", ex, item.download))
278.                     {
279.                         throw;
280.                     }
281.                 }
282.             }
283.         }
284. 
285.         private void DownloadProgress(DownloadOperationItem item)
286.         {
287.             BackgroundDownloadProgress currentProgress = item.download.Progress;
288.             BackgroundTransferStatus status = currentProgress.Status;
289.             int percentComplete = 0;
290. 
291.             if (currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive > 0)
292.             {
293.                 percentComplete = (int)((currentProgress.BytesReceived * 100) / currentProgress.TotalBytesToReceive);
294.             }
295. 
296.             // TODO[migrate-014]: see PATTERNS.md#threading
297.             var ignore = this.Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
298.             {
299.                 item.stateText = status.ToString();
300.                 item.percentComplete = percentComplete;
301.             });
302.         }
303. 
304.         private bool IsWebException(string title, Exception ex, DownloadOperation download = null)
305.         {
306.             WebErrorStatus error = BackgroundTransferError.GetStatus(ex.HResult);
307.             bool result = (error != WebErrorStatus.Unknown);
308.             string message = result ? error.ToString() : ex.Message;
309. 
310.             if (download == null)
311.             {
312.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
313.             }
314.             else
315.             {
316.                 rootPage.NotifyUser($"{download.ResultFile.Name} - {title}: {message}", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
317.             }

11. 
12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Globalization;
15. using System.Linq;
16. using System.Text;
17. using System.Threading.Tasks;
18. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Background;
19. using Windows.Data.Xml.Dom;
20. using Windows.Networking.BackgroundTransfer;
21. using Windows.Storage;
22. using Windows.UI.Notifications;
23. 
24. namespace Tasks
25. {
26.     // TODO[migrate-015]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
27.     public sealed class CompletionGroupTask : IBackgroundTask
28.     {
29.         // Completion groups allow us to immediately take action after a set of downloads completes.
30.         // In this sample, the server intentionally replies with an error status for some of the downloads.
31.         // Based on the trigger details, we can determine which of the downloads have failed and try them again
32.         // using a new completion group.
33.         public async void Run(IBackgroundTaskInstance taskInstance)
34.         {
35.             // The background task awaits on an asynchronous task (RetryDownloadsAsync). Take a deferral
36.             // to delay the background task from closing prematurely while the asynchronous code is still running.
37.             BackgroundTaskDeferral deferral = taskInstance.GetDeferral();
38. 
39.             BackgroundTransferCompletionGroupTriggerDetails details = taskInstance.TriggerDetails
40.                 as BackgroundTransferCompletionGroupTriggerDetails;
41. 
42.             if (details == null)
43.             {
44.                 // This task was not triggered by a completion group.
45.                 deferral.Complete();
46.                 return;
47.             }
48. 
49.             List<DownloadOperation> failedDownloads = new List<DownloadOperation>();
50.             int succeeded = 0;
51.             foreach (DownloadOperation download in details.Downloads)
52.             {
53.                 if (IsFailed(download))
54.                 {
55.                     failedDownloads.Add(download);
56.                 }
57.                 else
58.                 {
59.                     succeeded++;
60.                 }
61.             }
62. 
63.             InvokeSimpleToast(succeeded, failedDownloads.Count);
64. 
65.             if (failedDownloads.Count > 0)
66.             {
67.                 await RetryDownloadsAsync(failedDownloads);
68.             }
69. 
70.             deferral.Complete();
71.         }
72. 
73.         private bool IsFailed(DownloadOperation download)
74.         {
75.             BackgroundTransferStatus status = download.Progress.Status;
76.             if (status == BackgroundTransferStatus.Error || status == BackgroundTransferStatus.Canceled)
77.             {
78.                 return true;
79.             }
80. 
81.             ResponseInformation response = download.GetResponseInformation();
82.             if (response == null || response.StatusCode != 200)
83.             {
84.                 return true;
85.             }
86. 
87.             return false;
88.         }
89.         private async Task RetryDownloadsAsync(IEnumerable<DownloadOperation> downloadsToRetry)
90.         {
91.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = CompletionGroupTask.CreateBackgroundDownloader();
92. 
93.             foreach (DownloadOperation downloadToRetry in downloadsToRetry)
94.             {
95.                 // Retry with the same uri, but save to a different file name.
96.                 string originalName = downloadToRetry.ResultFile.Name;
97.                 string newName = originalName.Insert(originalName.LastIndexOf('.'), "_retried");
98.                 await downloadToRetry.ResultFile.RenameAsync(newName, NameCollisionOption.ReplaceExisting);
99. 
100.                 DownloadOperation download = downloader.CreateDownload(downloadToRetry.RequestedUri, downloadToRetry.ResultFile);
101. 
102.                 // We do not await on background transfer asynchronous tasks, since await will only 
103.                 // finish after the entire transfer is complete, which may take a very long time. 
104.                 // The completion of these downloads will be handled by a future CompletionGroupTask instance.
105.                 Task<DownloadOperation> startTask = download.StartAsync().AsTask();
106.             }
107. 
108.             downloader.CompletionGroup.Enable();
109.         }
110. 
111.         public void InvokeSimpleToast(int succeeded, int failed)
112.         {
113.             // TODO[migrate-016]: see PATTERNS.md#notifications
114.             XmlDocument toastXml = ToastNotificationManager.GetTemplateContent(ToastTemplateType.ToastImageAndText02);
115. 
116.             XmlNodeList stringElements = toastXml.GetElementsByTagName("text");
117.             stringElements.Item(0).AppendChild(toastXml.CreateTextNode(String.Format(
118.                 CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
119.                 "{0} downloads succeeded.",
120.                 succeeded)));
121.             stringElements.Item(1).AppendChild(toastXml.CreateTextNode(String.Format(
122.                 CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
123.                 "{0} downloads failed.",
124.                 failed)));
125. 
126.             ToastNotification toast = new ToastNotification(toastXml);
127.             // TODO[migrate-017]: see PATTERNS.md#notifications
128.             ToastNotificationManager.CreateToastNotifier().Show(toast);
129.         }
130. 
131.         public static BackgroundDownloader CreateBackgroundDownloader()
132.         {
133.             BackgroundTransferCompletionGroup completionGroup = new BackgroundTransferCompletionGroup();
134. 
135.             // TODO[migrate-018]: see PATTERNS.md#background-tasks
136.             BackgroundTaskBuilder builder = new BackgroundTaskBuilder();
137.             builder.TaskEntryPoint = "Tasks.CompletionGroupTask";
138.             builder.SetTrigger(completionGroup.Trigger);
139. 
140.             // The system automatically unregisters the BackgroundTransferCompletionGroup task when it triggers.
141.             // You do not need to unregister it explicitly.
142.             BackgroundTaskRegistration taskRegistration = builder.Register();
143. 
144.             BackgroundDownloader downloader = new BackgroundDownloader(completionGroup);
145. 
146.             return downloader;
147.         }
148. 
149.     }

{"file_text":"<?xml version=\"1.0\" encoding=\"utf-8\" ?>\n<Page\n    x:Name=\"DownloadPage\"\n    x:Class=\"SDKTemplate.Scenario1_Download\"\n    xmlns=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation\"\n    xmlns:x=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml\"\n    xmlns:local=\"using:SDKTemplate\"\n    xmlns:d=\"http://schemas.microsoft.com/expression/blend/2008\"\n    xmlns:mc=\"http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/markup-compatibility/2006\"\n    mc:Ignorable=\"d\">\n    <ScrollViewer HorizontalScrollMode=\"Disabled\" VerticalScrollMode=\"Enabled\"\n                  HorizontalScrollBarVisibility=\"Disabled\" VerticalScrollBarVisibility=\"Auto\">\n        <StackPanel x:Name=\"LayoutRoot\" Padding=\"12\">\n            <TextBlock TextWrapping=\"Wrap\" Style=\"{StaticResource BasicTextStyle}\">\n                When downloading a file from a server, application developers must\n                ensure they receive user consent for the download.
